- **find-opps**: Find new directions for an ops project — adjacent opportunities, strategic gaps, preparatory research. Use when known work is well-mined and you need to look outward. ([OdinMB/ops-workflow](https://github.com/OdinMB/ops-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/find-opps) · ★6)
- **find-optimizations**: Use when the user asks for high-value performance optimizations in the checklist app — order-of-magnitude wins in algorithms that matter, not micro-tuning. Surveys the hot paths (pure domain operations over templates/checklists/items, share-payload encode/decode, storage (de)serialization, per-render passes in the UI), presents candidates with complexity and trigger context for the user to gate the work, and is honest when the codebase has already been tuned and no high-value wins remain. ([niclaslindstedt/checklist](https://github.com/niclaslindstedt/checklist/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/find-optimizations) · ★1)
